##########
# The Red Keep
##########
#Aegonfort
the_red_keep_01 = {
	construction_time = very_quick_construction_time

	type_icon = "icon_structure_red_keep.dds"

	can_construct_potential = {
		building_requirement_tribal = no
	}

	is_enabled = {
		agot_generic_is_special_building_enabled = yes
	}

	cost_gold = expensive_building_tier_1_cost

	next_building = the_red_keep_02

	effect_desc = feast_cost_discount_max_desc

	character_modifier = {
		legitimacy_gain_mult = 0.01
		court_grandeur_baseline_add = -5
		monthly_prestige_gain_mult = 0.1
	}

	max_garrison = normal_building_max_garrison_tier_2

	province_modifier = {
		fort_level = 1
		defender_holding_advantage = 2
	}

	county_modifier = {
		county_opinion_add = 5
	}

	ai_value = {
		base = 100
		culture_likely_to_fortify_modifier = yes
	}

	type = special

	flag = travel_point_of_interest_martial
}

seven_gates_01 = {
	construction_time = very_slow_construction_time

	type_icon = "icon_structure_walls_of_kings_landing.dds"

	can_construct_potential = {
		barony = title:b_the_red_keep
		building_requirement_tribal = no
	}

	is_enabled = {
		agot_generic_is_special_building_enabled = yes
	}

	cost_gold = expensive_building_tier_6_cost

	max_garrison = normal_building_max_garrison_tier_6

	duchy_capital_county_modifier = {
		levy_size = 0.15
		tax_mult = normal_building_tax_mult_tier_2
	}

	county_modifier = {
		development_growth_factor = 0.1
		hostile_raid_time = 0.5
		fort_level = 3
		garrison_size = 0.5
	}

	ai_value = {
		base = 100
		culture_likely_to_fortify_modifier = yes
		modifier = { # Fill all building slots before going for special buildings
			factor = 0
			free_building_slots > 0
		}
	}

	type = duchy_capital
}

		monthly_county_control_decline_factor = 2.50

# Great Sept of Baelor

holy_site_great_sept_01 = {
	construction_time = very_slow_construction_time

	type_icon = "icon_structure_great_sept_of_baelor.dds"

	can_construct_potential = {
		building_requirement_tribal = no
	}

	is_enabled = {
		agot_generic_is_special_building_enabled = yes
		custom_description = {
			text = agot_holy_site_fallback_trigger
			OR = {
				scope:holder = {
					religion = religion:the_seven_religion
				}
				scope:holder = {
					religion = religion:the_pact_religion
				}
				custom_description = {
					text = holy_site_building_trigger
					barony = {
						is_holy_site_of = scope:holder.faith
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	asset = {
		type = pdxmesh
		name = "building_special_KL_BaelorsSept_mesh"
	}

	cost_gold = expensive_building_tier_7_cost

	effect_desc = royal_garden_effect_desc

	county_holder_character_modifier = {
		monthly_piety = 0.5
		intrigue_per_piety_level = 1
		clergy_opinion = 5
	}

	character_modifier = {
		monthly_piety = 0.5
		piety_level_impact_mult = 0.5
		monthly_piety_from_buildings_mult = 0.1
	}

	county_modifier = {
		tax_mult = good_building_tax_mult_tier_4
		development_growth_factor = 0.2
	}

	province_modifier = {
		monthly_income = excellent_building_tax_tier_5
	}

	ai_value = {
		base = 100
		modifier = { # Fill all building slots before going for special buildings
			factor = 0
			free_building_slots > 0
		}
	}

	type = special

	flag = travel_point_of_interest_religious
	flag = holy_building
}
generic_great_sept_01 = {
	construction_time = very_slow_construction_time

	type_icon = "icon_structure_great_sept_of_baelor.dds"

	can_construct_potential = {
		building_requirement_tribal = no
		NAND = {
			game_start_date = 8170.12.11 #Event constructing it in story cycle
			current_date <= 8176.1.1 #Failsafe
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		scope:holder = { has_trait = zealous }
		scope:holder = { piety_level >= 5 }
		scope:holder = { has_primary_title = title:h_the_iron_throne }
		county.faith.religion = { this = religion:the_seven_religion  }
	}

	is_enabled = {
		agot_generic_is_special_building_enabled = yes
		custom_description = {
			text = agot_holy_site_fallback_trigger
			OR = {
				scope:holder = {
					religion = religion:the_seven_religion
				}
				scope:holder = {
					religion = religion:the_pact_religion
				}
				custom_description = {
					text = holy_site_building_trigger
					barony = {
						is_holy_site_of = scope:holder.faith
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	asset = {
		type = pdxmesh
		name = "building_special_KL_BaelorsSept_mesh"
	}

	cost_gold = expensive_building_tier_7_cost

	effect_desc = royal_garden_effect_desc

	county_holder_character_modifier = {
		monthly_piety = 0.5
		intrigue_per_piety_level = 1
		clergy_opinion = 5
	}

	character_modifier = {
		monthly_piety = 0.5
		piety_level_impact_mult = 0.5
		monthly_piety_from_buildings_mult = 0.1
	}

	county_modifier = {
		tax_mult = good_building_tax_mult_tier_4
		development_growth_factor = 0.2
	}

	province_modifier = {
		monthly_income = excellent_building_tax_tier_5
	}

	ai_value = {
		base = 100
		modifier = { # Fill all building slots before going for special buildings
			factor = 0
			free_building_slots > 0
		}
	}

	type = special

	flag = travel_point_of_interest_religious
	flag = holy_building
}

# Fully Restored & Functional Version
dragonpit_ruins_03 = {

	construction_time = very_slow_construction_time

	type_icon = "icon_building_dragonpit.dds"

	can_construct = {
		agot_can_build_or_restore_dragonpits = yes

		county.holder = {

		stationed_archers_toughness_mult = very_high_maa_toughness_tier_2
		supply_limit_mult = 1.5
	}

moat_cailin_02 = {

	construction_time = very_slow_construction_time

	type_icon = "icon_structure_moat_cailin.dds"

	can_construct_potential = {
		building_requirement_tribal = no
	}

	can_construct = {
		OR = {
			title:c_moat_cailin.holder = {
				is_human = yes
				agot_is_independent_ruler = yes
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = moat_cailin_top_liege_desc
				title:c_moat_cailin.holder.top_liege ?= {
					has_title = title:e_the_north
					agot_is_independent_ruler = yes
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = moat_cailin_fortification_rights_desc
				title:e_the_north.holder ?= {
					OR = {
						vassal_contract_has_flag = fortification_rights_granted
						has_variable = mw_obl_fortification_rights_granted
					}
				}
				OR = {
					title:e_the_north.holder = title:c_moat_cailin.holder
					title:c_moat_cailin.holder = {
						any_liege_or_above = {
							this = title:e_the_north.holder
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	asset = {
		type = pdxmesh
		name = "building_special_moat_cailin_02_mesh"
	}

	next_building = moat_cailin_03

	on_start = {
		county.holder = {
			add_character_flag = rebuilding_moat_cailin
			add_character_flag = started_rebuilding_moat_cailin
			trigger_event = agot_ruins.3000
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = constructor
			value = scope:character
		}
		title:c_ruins.holder = {
			trigger_event = {
				id = agot_rebuilding_ruins.1000
				months = { 11 37 }
			}
		}
	}

	on_cancelled = {
		root = { save_scope_as = province }
		county.holder = {
			add_character_flag = cancelled_cailin
			trigger_event = agot_ruins.9998
		}
	}

	on_complete = {
		county.holder = {
			trigger_event = agot_ruins.3001
		}
		remove_variable = constructor
	}

	is_enabled = {
		agot_generic_is_special_building_enabled = yes
	}

	cost_gold = expensive_building_tier_4_cost

	character_modifier = {
		domain_limit = 1
		monthly_prestige_gain_mult = 0.05
		legitimacy_gain_mult = 0.05
	}

	max_garrison = normal_building_max_garrison_tier_1

	province_modifier = {
		defender_holding_advantage = 10
		fort_level = 10
		stationed_archers_damage_mult = very_high_maa_damage_tier_3
		stationed_archers_toughness_mult = very_high_maa_toughness_tier_3
		supply_limit_mult = 1.75
	}
	county_modifier = {
		hostile_raid_time = 0.4
		travel_danger = -5
	}

	ai_value = {
		base = 100
		culture_likely_to_fortify_modifier = yes
	}

	type = special

	flag = travel_point_of_interest_martial
}

moat_cailin_03 = {

	construction_time = very_slow_construction_time

	type_icon = "icon_structure_moat_cailin.dds"

	can_construct_potential = {
		building_requirement_tribal = no
	}

	can_construct = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = title:c_moat_cailin.holder
				title:c_moat_cailin.holder = { agot_is_independent_ruler = yes }
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = moat_cailin_top_liege_desc
				AND = {
					exists = title:e_the_north.holder
					title:e_the_north.holder = title:c_moat_cailin.holder.top_liege
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = moat_cailin_fortification_rights_desc
				AND = {
					exists = title:e_the_north.holder
					title:e_the_north.holder = { vassal_contract_has_flag = fortification_rights_granted }
					OR = {
						title:e_the_north.holder = title:c_moat_cailin.holder
						title:c_moat_cailin.holder = {
							any_liege_or_above = {
								this = title:e_the_north.holder
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	asset = {
		type = pdxmesh
		name = "building_special_moat_cailin_03_mesh"
	}

	on_start = {
		county.holder = {
			add_character_flag = rebuilding_moat_cailin
			add_character_flag = started_rebuilding_moat_cailin
			trigger_event = agot_ruins.3002
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = constructor
			value = scope:character
		}
		title:c_ruins.holder = {
			trigger_event = {
				id = agot_rebuilding_ruins.1000
				months = { 11 37 }
			}
		}
	}

	on_cancelled = {
		root = { save_scope_as = province }
		county.holder = {
			add_character_flag = cancelled_cailin
			trigger_event = agot_ruins.9998
		}
	}

	on_complete = {
		county.holder = {
			trigger_event = agot_ruins.3003
		}
		remove_variable = constructor
	}

	is_enabled = {
		agot_generic_is_special_building_enabled = yes
	}

	cost_gold = expensive_building_tier_8_cost

	character_modifier = {
		domain_limit = 1
		monthly_prestige_gain_mult = 0.1
		legitimacy_gain_mult = 0.1
	}

	max_garrison = normal_building_max_garrison_tier_4

	province_modifier = {
		defender_holding_advantage = 10
		fort_level = 15
		levy_size = 0.25
		stationed_archers_damage_mult = very_high_maa_damage_tier_5
		stationed_archers_toughness_mult = very_high_maa_toughness_tier_5
		supply_limit_mult = 2.0
		garrison_size = 0.25

	county_modifier = {
